## eduwiki

- [About this document](/raw/regulations/about-this-document.md): Overview of the document summarizing the educational process at Innopolis University, including update procedures and annual version freezing.
- [Innopolis University Manifesto on Medium of Instruction](/raw/regulations/innopolisuniversitymanifestoonmedium.md): Defines English as the primary medium of instruction for all academic activities while allowing other languages in informal or private contexts.
- [Overall Regulations](/raw/regulations/overallregulations.md): A collection of links to key university documents including the medium of instruction manifesto, degree structures, and course lecturing guidelines.
